## ✅ PRODUCT 1 — LoanData Canon (BUILD + TEST ONLY)

You want an **enterprise “canonical loan record” kernel**: one regulator-safe source of truth that can be **proven**, **replayed**, **audited**, **versioned**, and **consumed** by downstream products (CovenantPulse, GreenLedger, TradeReady, PortView) without argument.

---

# A) Business Reality (Loan Market Pain)

## A1. Exact problem statement (real pain)
In real lending shops (banks, servicers, funds), the same loan exists simultaneously as:

- **Origination system view** (what was booked)
- **Servicing system view** (what is being collected)
- **Risk view** (what models think the exposure is)
- **Covenant/credit file view** (what legal docs say)
- **Treasury view** (cashflows and interest accrual)
- **Secondary market tape view** (what is disclosed to buyers)

These views **do not match** because each system uses different identifiers, different timing rules, partial fields, and different “truth moments”.

**LoanData Canon** creates a **single canonical loan snapshot + immutable versions** and guarantees:
- every change is traceable (who/when/why/source),
- every derived number can be reproduced,
- every consumer gets consistent data.

## A2. Why this pain exists today
- Loan data is updated by **many actors**: ops, servicing, credit, legal, syndications, agents, borrowers, external vendors.
- Data arrives in **inconsistent formats**: agent tapes, servicer exports, PDFs, emails, spreadsheets.
- Many systems are **append-only in some places and overwrite in others**, so history is lost.
- Identifiers differ: facility ID vs loan ID vs tranche ID vs agent reference vs internal booking reference.
- Timing rules differ: EOD vs intraday; accrual basis; FX conversion cutoffs.

## A3. Who suffers (roles/departments)
- **Credit Risk**: wrong exposure, wrong PD/LGD inputs.
- **Loan Operations / Servicing Ops**: reconciliations, breaks, manual fixes.
- **Finance / Treasury**: incorrect accruals, interest calculations, cash forecasting.
- **Legal / Covenant teams**: cannot trust covenant fields, inconsistent terms.
- **Syndications / Secondary market**: bad tapes, disputes, failed settlements.
- **Internal Audit / Regulators**: cannot show lineage and control evidence.

## A4. Quantified impact (time / cost / risk)
Typical large lender outcomes:
- **20–60 minutes per loan per month** of manual reconciliation in ops (breaks, duplicates, missing fields).
- **Material settlement risk** in secondary trading when data mismatches lead to trade breaks.
- **Regulatory risk**: inability to reproduce reported exposures at a given date (“prove what you knew when”).
- **Downstream waste**: every product team builds its own mapping layer → inconsistent logic.

---

# A2) Why existing systems fail (specific)
1. **Overwrites instead of versioning**: you lose historical terms and cannot reproduce past reports.
2. **No canonical identifier strategy**: merges/splits/tranches create broken references.
3. **No deterministic lineage**: cannot say “this field came from this source file at this time”.
4. **Weak validation contracts**: downstream teams interpret nulls differently.
5. **No eventing contract**: downstream systems poll or scrape, leading to drift.

---

# B) Enterprise Requirements (Numbered + Acceptance Criteria)

> Naming: **LDC** = LoanData Canon  
> Format: **R-LDC-XX**

### R-LDC-01 Canonical Loan Identity
**Requirement:** System shall maintain a canonical loan identity that links all source identifiers.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Given source IDs from multiple systems, system creates/links to one **canonical_loan_id**.
- Supports multiple identifiers per loan with source/system type and validity window.
- Rejects ambiguous mapping unless resolved by workflow (manual resolution endpoint).

### R-LDC-02 Immutable Versioning of Canonical Loan Snapshot
**Requirement:** System shall store canonical loan snapshots as immutable versions.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Any change produces a new version row; prior versions remain unchanged.
- Each version has: version number, effective timestamp, created timestamp, created by, reason, source reference.
- Ability to retrieve snapshot “as-of” a timestamp.

### R-LDC-03 Field-Level Lineage
**Requirement:** System shall record lineage for each canonical field.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- For each version, for each field: source system, source record reference, confidence, transform rule id.
- Auditors can query lineage for a field in any version.

### R-LDC-04 Deterministic Validation + Rejection
**Requirement:** Canonicalization must enforce deterministic validation rules and reject invalid input.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Invalid currency codes / negative principal / invalid dates rejected with explicit error codes.
- Validation failures stored as immutable validation events with payload hash.

### R-LDC-05 Data Mutability Rules
**Requirement:** System shall classify data into immutable vs mutable and enforce it.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Immutable terms (e.g., original agreement date) cannot be changed without override workflow reason code.
- Mutable fields (e.g., outstanding principal) can change with normal ingestion.
- Overrides are fully logged and require explicit “override_reason”.

### R-LDC-06 Ingestion Idempotency
**Requirement:** System shall ensure ingestion is idempotent per source message/file record.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Replaying same message does not create new versions.
- Dedup uses (source_system, source_message_id, payload_hash).

### R-LDC-07 Outbox Event Publishing
**Requirement:** Each published canonical version must emit an event for downstream consumption.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Event persisted in outbox in same DB transaction as version write.
- Downstream can consume via Kafka-compatible event payload or HTTP polling.
- Exactly-once effect at consumer achievable via event_id.

### R-LDC-08 Security + PII Controls
**Requirement:** APIs require authentication; sensitive fields protected.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Role-based access (READ, WRITE, AUDIT).
- Audit endpoints restricted.
- PII fields (borrower name) masked unless privileged role.

### R-LDC-09 Audit Evidence Pack
**Requirement:** Provide regulator/auditor evidence endpoints.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- “Why this value?” endpoint returns lineage + rule + source record.
- “What changed?” endpoint returns field diff between versions.
- Exportable evidence JSON.

### R-LDC-10 Performance
**Requirement:** Query “current snapshot” within SLA.  
**Acceptance criteria:**
- Current snapshot retrieval for a loan returns < 200ms at p95 (DB indexed).
- As-of retrieval uses indexes on effective_ts.

# C) Domain Model (Entities + Fields + Why + What breaks)

## C1. Entity list
1. **CanonicalLoan**
2. **LoanIdentifier**
3. **LoanVersion**
4. **LoanFieldLineage**
5. **IngestionReceipt**
6. **ValidationEvent**
7. **OutboxEvent**

## C2. Field-level explanation

### 1) CanonicalLoan
- `canonical_loan_id (UUID)` — primary identity.  
  **Why:** everything downstream keys off this.  
  **If missing:** duplicates across systems; trades/covenants mis-join.
- `status (ACTIVE/CLOSED/DEFAULTED)` — loan lifecycle state.  
  **If missing:** downstream reporting errors.
- `created_at, created_by` — governance.

### 2) LoanIdentifier
- `source_system` — ORIGINATION/SERVICER/AGENT_TAPE/etc.  
- `source_identifier` — raw ID value.  
- `valid_from, valid_to` — supports changes (renumbering).  
  **If missing:** identity drift; cannot reconcile.

### 3) LoanVersion (immutable snapshots)
Core canonical terms + state at time:
- `version_no` — monotonic per loan.
- `effective_ts` — business effective time.
- `principal_outstanding` — current.
- `original_principal` — immutable-ish (override required).
- `currency` — ISO code.
- `interest_rate_type` (FIXED/FLOATING)
- `base_rate_index` (SOFR/SONIA/EURIBOR)
- `spread_bps`
- `maturity_date`
- `payment_frequency`
- `day_count_convention`
- `borrower_lei` (or internal legal entity id)
- `facility_type` (TERM_LOAN/RCF)
- `governing_law`
- `created_at, created_by, reason_code, source_ref`
**If missing:** you cannot reproduce “truth at time”, cannot audit.

### 4) LoanFieldLineage
- `field_name`
- `source_system`
- `source_record_ref`
- `transform_rule_id`
- `confidence_score`
**If missing:** cannot defend numbers to auditors.

### 5) IngestionReceipt
- `(source_system, source_message_id, payload_hash)` unique  
- `received_at`, `processed_at`, `status`
**If missing:** replay duplicates, inconsistent versions.

### 6) ValidationEvent
- `event_id`, `canonical_loan_id (optional)`, `source_ref`
- `error_code`, `details_json`, `payload_hash`, `created_at`
**If missing:** no evidence of control failures.

### 7) OutboxEvent
- `event_id`, `event_type`, `aggregate_id`, `payload_json`, `created_at`, `published_at`
**If missing:** downstream drift and polling chaos.

---

# D) Database Layer (PostgreSQL DDL + constraints + immutability rules)

> **Immutable vs mutable**  
> - **LoanVersion rows are immutable** after insert (enforced by app + optional DB trigger).  
> - Current state is derived: the “latest version” (max version_no).

```sql
-- schema: loan_data_canon

CREATE TABLE canonical_loan (
  canonical_loan_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE loan_identifier (
  loan_identifier_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  canonical_loan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES canonical_loan(canonical_loan_id),
  source_system VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  source_identifier VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  valid_to TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_identifier UNIQUE (source_system, source_identifier, valid_to)
);

CREATE TABLE loan_version (
  loan_version_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  canonical_loan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES canonical_loan(canonical_loan_id),
  version_no INT NOT NULL,
  effective_ts TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  reason_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  source_ref VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

  original_principal NUMERIC(20,2) NOT NULL,
  principal_outstanding NUMERIC(20,2) NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

  interest_rate_type VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
  base_rate_index VARCHAR(20) NULL,
  spread_bps INT NULL,

  maturity_date DATE NOT NULL,
  payment_frequency VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  day_count_convention VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,

  borrower_lei VARCHAR(50) NULL,
  facility_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  governing_law VARCHAR(50) NULL,

  CONSTRAINT uq_loan_version UNIQUE (canonical_loan_id, version_no),
  CONSTRAINT ck_principal_nonneg CHECK (principal_outstanding >= 0 AND original_principal >= 0),
  CONSTRAINT ck_currency_len CHECK (char_length(currency) = 3)
);

CREATE INDEX idx_loan_version_latest ON loan_version(canonical_loan_id, version_no DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_loan_version_asof ON loan_version(canonical_loan_id, effective_ts DESC);

CREATE TABLE loan_field_lineage (
  lineage_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  loan_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES loan_version(loan_version_id),
  field_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  source_system VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  source_record_ref VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  transform_rule_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  confidence_score NUMERIC(5,4) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT uq_lineage UNIQUE (loan_version_id, field_name)
);

CREATE TABLE ingestion_receipt (
  ingestion_receipt_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  source_system VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  source_message_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- RECEIVED/PROCESSED/REJECTED
  error_code VARCHAR(50) NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_ingestion UNIQUE (source_system, source_message_id, payload_hash)
);

CREATE TABLE validation_event (
  validation_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  canonical_loan_id UUID NULL,
  source_ref VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  payload_hash VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  error_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  details_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE outbox_event (
  event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  aggregate_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,
  payload_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  publish_attempts INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_unpublished ON outbox_event(published_at) WHERE published_at IS NULL;
```

**Why each table exists** is exactly mapped to the domain entities above.  
**What is immutable vs mutable**:
- `loan_version`, `loan_field_lineage`, `validation_event`, `outbox_event` → **append-only** (immutable)
- `canonical_loan.status` may change (ACTIVE→CLOSED) but logged via a new version event (recommended).

## E1. Domain entities (JPA)

### CanonicalLoan.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

@Entity
@Table(name = "canonical_loan")
public class CanonicalLoan {
    @Id
    @Column(name = "canonical_loan_id", nullable = false)
    private UUID id;

    @Column(nullable = false)
    private String status;

    @Column(name="created_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    @Column(name="created_by", nullable = false)
    private String createdBy;

    protected CanonicalLoan() {}

    public CanonicalLoan(UUID id, String status, Instant createdAt, String createdBy) {
        this.id = id;
        this.status = status;
        this.createdAt = createdAt;
        this.createdBy = createdBy;
    }

    public UUID getId() { return id; }
    public String getStatus() { return status; }
    public Instant getCreatedAt() { return createdAt; }
    public String getCreatedBy() { return createdBy; }

    public void setStatus(String status) { this.status = status; }
}
```

### LoanVersion.java (immutable snapshot)
```java
package com.bank.ldc.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.UUID;

@Entity
@Table(name = "loan_version",
       uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(name="uq_loan_version", columnNames={"canonical_loan_id","version_no"}))
public class LoanVersion {
    @Id
    @Column(name="loan_version_id", nullable=false)
    private UUID id;

    @Column(name="canonical_loan_id", nullable=false)
    private UUID canonicalLoanId;

    @Column(name="version_no", nullable=false)
    private int versionNo;

    @Column(name="effective_ts", nullable=false)
    private Instant effectiveTs;

    @Column(name="created_at", nullable=false)
    private Instant createdAt;

    @Column(name="created_by", nullable=false)
    private String createdBy;

    @Column(name="reason_code", nullable=false)
    private String reasonCode;

    @Column(name="source_ref", nullable=false)
    private String sourceRef;

    @Column(name="original_principal", nullable=false, precision=20, scale=2)
    private BigDecimal originalPrincipal;

    @Column(name="principal_outstanding", nullable=false, precision=20, scale=2)
    private BigDecimal principalOutstanding;

    @Column(nullable=false, length=3)
    private String currency;

    @Column(name="interest_rate_type", nullable=false)
    private String interestRateType;

    @Column(name="base_rate_index")
    private String baseRateIndex;

    @Column(name="spread_bps")
    private Integer spreadBps;

    @Column(name="maturity_date", nullable=false)
    private LocalDate maturityDate;

    @Column(name="payment_frequency", nullable=false)
    private String paymentFrequency;

    @Column(name="day_count_convention", nullable=false)
    private String dayCountConvention;

    @Column(name="borrower_lei")
    private String borrowerLei;

    @Column(name="facility_type", nullable=false)
    private String facilityType;

    @Column(name="governing_law")
    private String governingLaw;

    protected LoanVersion() {}

    public LoanVersion(UUID id, UUID canonicalLoanId, int versionNo, Instant effectiveTs,
                       Instant createdAt, String createdBy, String reasonCode, String sourceRef,
                       BigDecimal originalPrincipal, BigDecimal principalOutstanding, String currency,
                       String interestRateType, String baseRateIndex, Integer spreadBps,
                       LocalDate maturityDate, String paymentFrequency, String dayCountConvention,
                       String borrowerLei, String facilityType, String governingLaw) {
        this.id = id;
        this.canonicalLoanId = canonicalLoanId;
        this.versionNo = versionNo;
        this.effectiveTs = effectiveTs;
        this.createdAt = createdAt;
        this.createdBy = createdBy;
        this.reasonCode = reasonCode;
        this.sourceRef = sourceRef;
        this.originalPrincipal = originalPrincipal;
        this.principalOutstanding = principalOutstanding;
        this.currency = currency;
        this.interestRateType = interestRateType;
        this.baseRateIndex = baseRateIndex;
        this.spreadBps = spreadBps;
        this.maturityDate = maturityDate;
        this.paymentFrequency = paymentFrequency;
        this.dayCountConvention = dayCountConvention;
        this.borrowerLei = borrowerLei;
        this.facilityType = facilityType;
        this.governingLaw = governingLaw;
    }

    public UUID getId() { return id; }
    public UUID getCanonicalLoanId() { return canonicalLoanId; }
    public int getVersionNo() { return versionNo; }
    public Instant getEffectiveTs() { return effectiveTs; }
    public BigDecimal getPrincipalOutstanding() { return principalOutstanding; }
    public BigDecimal getOriginalPrincipal() { return originalPrincipal; }
    public String getCurrency() { return currency; }
    // other getters omitted for brevity (keep in codebase)
}
```

(You will implement similar JPA entities for `LoanIdentifier`, `LoanFieldLineage`, `IngestionReceipt`, `ValidationEvent`, `OutboxEvent` — same pattern, no shortcuts.)

## E2. DTO: Ingest request (strict contract)

```java
package com.bank.ldc.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

public class IngestLoanRequest {
    @NotBlank public String sourceSystem;
    @NotBlank public String sourceMessageId;
    @NotBlank public String sourceRef;            // e.g., "agent_tape_2026-01-12.csv#row=8821"
    @NotBlank public String createdBy;            // user/service id

    // identity from source
    @NotBlank public String sourceLoanIdentifier;

    // canonical payload
    @NotNull public Instant effectiveTs;
    @NotNull @DecimalMin("0.00") public BigDecimal originalPrincipal;
    @NotNull @DecimalMin("0.00") public BigDecimal principalOutstanding;
    @NotBlank @Size(min=3,max=3) public String currency;

    @NotBlank public String interestRateType;     // FIXED/FLOATING
    public String baseRateIndex;                  // SOFR/SONIA/EURIBOR
    public Integer spreadBps;

    @NotNull public LocalDate maturityDate;
    @NotBlank public String paymentFrequency;
    @NotBlank public String dayCountConvention;

    public String borrowerLei;
    @NotBlank public String facilityType;
    public String governingLaw;

    // override control
    public String overrideReason; // required if attempting to change immutable-ish fields
}
```

## E3. Core services (deterministic, regulator-safe)

### IdempotencyService.java
**Responsibility:** prevent duplicate processing.  
**Inputs:** sourceSystem, sourceMessageId, payloadHash  
**Outputs:** whether to process, and records receipt  
**Failure modes:** DB unavailable, unique constraint violation (treated as duplicate)

```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.IngestionReceipt;
import com.bank.ldc.repo.IngestionReceiptRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

@Service
public class IdempotencyService {
    private final IngestionReceiptRepository repo;

    public IdempotencyService(IngestionReceiptRepository repo) {
        this.repo = repo;
    }

    @Transactional
    public boolean registerIfNew(String sourceSystem, String sourceMessageId, String payloadHash) {
        if (repo.existsBySourceSystemAndSourceMessageIdAndPayloadHash(sourceSystem, sourceMessageId, payloadHash)) {
            return false;
        }
        IngestionReceipt r = new IngestionReceipt(
                UUID.randomUUID(), sourceSystem, sourceMessageId, payloadHash,
                Instant.now(), null, "RECEIVED", null
        );
        repo.save(r);
        return true;
    }

    @Transactional
    public void markProcessed(String sourceSystem, String sourceMessageId, String payloadHash) {
        repo.markProcessed(sourceSystem, sourceMessageId, payloadHash, Instant.now(), "PROCESSED", null);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void markRejected(String sourceSystem, String sourceMessageId, String payloadHash, String errorCode) {
        repo.markProcessed(sourceSystem, sourceMessageId, payloadHash, Instant.now(), "REJECTED", errorCode);
    }
}
```

### ValidationService.java
**Responsibility:** deterministic rules; produces explicit error codes.  
**Failure modes:** none (pure logic)

```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.dto.IngestLoanRequest;
import com.bank.ldc.error.ErrorCodes;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Service
public class ValidationService {

    public List<String> validate(IngestLoanRequest req) {
        List<String> errors = new ArrayList<>();

        if (!req.currency.matches("^[A-Z]{3}$")) errors.add(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_CURRENCY_INVALID);

        if (req.interestRateType.equals("FLOATING")) {
            if (req.baseRateIndex == null || req.baseRateIndex.isBlank())
                errors.add(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_BASE_RATE_REQUIRED);
            if (req.spreadBps == null)
                errors.add(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_SPREAD_REQUIRED);
        }

        if (req.principalOutstanding.compareTo(req.originalPrincipal) > 0)
            errors.add(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_OUTSTANDING_GT_ORIGINAL);

        if (req.maturityDate.isBefore(req.effectiveTs.atZone(java.time.ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate()))
            errors.add(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_MATURITY_BEFORE_EFFECTIVE);

        return errors;
    }
}
```

### LoanCanonicalizationService.java
**Responsibility:** identity resolution + version creation + lineage + outbox event, all in one transaction.  
**Inputs:** IngestLoanRequest  
**Outputs:** canonical_loan_id + version_no  
**Failure modes:** validation failures, ambiguous ID mapping, DB failures

```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.*;
import com.bank.ldc.dto.IngestLoanRequest;
import com.bank.ldc.error.ApiException;
import com.bank.ldc.error.ErrorCodes;
import com.bank.ldc.repo.*;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

@Service
public class LoanCanonicalizationService {

    private final CanonicalLoanRepository loanRepo;
    private final LoanIdentifierRepository idRepo;
    private final LoanVersionRepository versionRepo;
    private final LoanFieldLineageRepository lineageRepo;
    private final ValidationEventRepository validationEventRepo;
    private final EventOutboxService outboxService;
    private final ValidationService validationService;

    public LoanCanonicalizationService(
            CanonicalLoanRepository loanRepo,
            LoanIdentifierRepository idRepo,
            LoanVersionRepository versionRepo,
            LoanFieldLineageRepository lineageRepo,
            ValidationEventRepository validationEventRepo,
            EventOutboxService outboxService,
            ValidationService validationService) {
        this.loanRepo = loanRepo;
        this.idRepo = idRepo;
        this.versionRepo = versionRepo;
        this.lineageRepo = lineageRepo;
        this.validationEventRepo = validationEventRepo;
        this.outboxService = outboxService;
        this.validationService = validationService;
    }

    @Transactional
    public CanonicalizationResult canonicalize(IngestLoanRequest req) {
        List<String> errors = validationService.validate(req);
        if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
            validationEventRepo.save(new ValidationEvent(
                    UUID.randomUUID(),
                    null,
                    req.sourceRef,
                    "payloadHashProvidedByController",
                    ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_FAILED,
                    Map.of("errors", errors),
                    Instant.now()
            ));
            throw new ApiException(ErrorCodes.VALIDATION_FAILED, "Validation failed: " + errors);
        }

        UUID canonicalLoanId = resolveOrCreateCanonicalLoan(req);

        int nextVersion = versionRepo.findMaxVersionNo(canonicalLoanId).orElse(0) + 1;

        // immutability enforcement example:
        Optional<LoanVersion> latest = versionRepo.findLatest(canonicalLoanId);
        if (latest.isPresent()) {
            boolean changingOriginalPrincipal =
                    latest.get().getOriginalPrincipal().compareTo(req.originalPrincipal) != 0;
            if (changingOriginalPrincipal && (req.overrideReason == null || req.overrideReason.isBlank())) {
                throw new ApiException(ErrorCodes.IMMUTABLE_FIELD_CHANGE_REQUIRES_OVERRIDE,
                        "Changing originalPrincipal requires overrideReason");
            }
        }

        LoanVersion v = new LoanVersion(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                canonicalLoanId,
                nextVersion,
                req.effectiveTs,
                Instant.now(),
                req.createdBy,
                "INGEST",
                req.sourceRef,
                req.originalPrincipal,
                req.principalOutstanding,
                req.currency,
                req.interestRateType,
                req.baseRateIndex,
                req.spreadBps,
                req.maturityDate,
                req.paymentFrequency,
                req.dayCountConvention,
                req.borrowerLei,
                req.facilityType,
                req.governingLaw
        );
        versionRepo.save(v);

        // field lineage (example for a few fields; in production do all canonical fields)
        lineageRepo.save(lineage("currency", v.getId(), req.sourceSystem, req.sourceRef, "RULE-CURRENCY-PASSTHRU", 0.9999));
        lineageRepo.save(lineage("principal_outstanding", v.getId(), req.sourceSystem, req.sourceRef, "RULE-PO-PASSTHRU", 0.9999));
        lineageRepo.save(lineage("maturity_date", v.getId(), req.sourceSystem, req.sourceRef, "RULE-MATURITY-PASSTHRU", 0.9999));

        // outbox event
        outboxService.enqueueLoanVersionPublished(canonicalLoanId, v.getId(), nextVersion);

        return new CanonicalizationResult(canonicalLoanId, nextVersion, v.getId());
    }

    private UUID resolveOrCreateCanonicalLoan(IngestLoanRequest req) {
        List<LoanIdentifier> matches = idRepo.findActiveBySourceSystemAndSourceIdentifier(req.sourceSystem, req.sourceLoanIdentifier);

        if (matches.size() > 1) {
            throw new ApiException(ErrorCodes.IDENTITY_AMBIGUOUS, "Multiple canonical loans match this identifier");
        }
        if (matches.size() == 1) return matches.get(0).getCanonicalLoanId();

        UUID newId = UUID.randomUUID();
        loanRepo.save(new CanonicalLoan(newId, "ACTIVE", Instant.now(), req.createdBy));
        idRepo.save(new LoanIdentifier(UUID.randomUUID(), newId, req.sourceSystem, req.sourceLoanIdentifier, Instant.now(), null, Instant.now(), req.createdBy));
        return newId;
    }

    private LoanFieldLineage lineage(String field, UUID loanVersionId, String sourceSystem, String sourceRef, String ruleId, double confidence) {
        return new LoanFieldLineage(UUID.randomUUID(), loanVersionId, field, sourceSystem, sourceRef, ruleId, confidence, Instant.now());
    }

    public record CanonicalizationResult(UUID canonicalLoanId, int versionNo, UUID loanVersionId) {}
}
```

### EventOutboxService.java
**Responsibility:** store event in outbox atomically with version.  
**Failure modes:** DB unavailable.

```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.OutboxEvent;
import com.bank.ldc.repo.OutboxEventRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

@Service
public class EventOutboxService {
    private final OutboxEventRepository repo;

    public EventOutboxService(OutboxEventRepository repo) {
        this.repo = repo;
    }

    public void enqueueLoanVersionPublished(UUID canonicalLoanId, UUID loanVersionId, int versionNo) {
        OutboxEvent e = new OutboxEvent(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                "LoanVersionPublished",
                "CanonicalLoan",
                canonicalLoanId,
                Map.of(
                        "canonicalLoanId", canonicalLoanId.toString(),
                        "loanVersionId", loanVersionId.toString(),
                        "versionNo", versionNo
                ),
                Instant.now(),
                null,
                0
        );
        repo.save(e);
    }
}
```

## E4. API Layer (Controllers)

### LoanIngestionController.java
**Responsibility:** ingestion endpoint, hashes payload, idempotency, calls canonicalization.  
**Failure modes:** duplicates, validation errors.

```java
package com.bank.ldc.api;

import com.bank.ldc.dto.IngestLoanRequest;
import com.bank.ldc.error.ApiException;
import com.bank.ldc.error.ErrorCodes;
import com.bank.ldc.service.IdempotencyService;
import com.bank.ldc.service.LoanCanonicalizationService;
import com.bank.ldc.util.Hashing;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/ingest")
public class LoanIngestionController {

    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;
    private final LoanCanonicalizationService canonService;

    public LoanIngestionController(IdempotencyService idempotencyService, LoanCanonicalizationService canonService) {
        this.idempotencyService = idempotencyService;
        this.canonService = canonService;
    }

    @PostMapping("/loan")
    public ResponseEntity<?> ingestLoan(@Valid @RequestBody IngestLoanRequest req) {
        String payloadHash = Hashing.sha256(req); // stable serialization in real impl
        boolean isNew = idempotencyService.registerIfNew(req.sourceSystem, req.sourceMessageId, payloadHash);
        if (!isNew) {
            return ResponseEntity.ok().body(java.util.Map.of("status", "DUPLICATE_IGNORED"));
        }

        try {
            var result = canonService.canonicalize(req);
            idempotencyService.markProcessed(req.sourceSystem, req.sourceMessageId, payloadHash);
            return ResponseEntity.ok().body(java.util.Map.of(
                    "status", "PROCESSED",
                    "canonicalLoanId", result.canonicalLoanId().toString(),
                    "versionNo", result.versionNo()
            ));
        } catch (ApiException ex) {
            idempotencyService.markRejected(req.sourceSystem, req.sourceMessageId, payloadHash, ex.getErrorCode());
            throw ex;
        }
    }
}
```

### LoanQueryController.java (current + as-of)
```java
package com.bank.ldc.api;

import com.bank.ldc.repo.LoanVersionRepository;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/loans")
public class LoanQueryController {

    private final LoanVersionRepository versionRepo;

    public LoanQueryController(LoanVersionRepository versionRepo) {
        this.versionRepo = versionRepo;
    }

    @GetMapping("/{canonicalLoanId}/current")
    public Object current(@PathVariable UUID canonicalLoanId) {
        return versionRepo.findLatest(canonicalLoanId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Loan not found"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{canonicalLoanId}/asof")
    public Object asOf(@PathVariable UUID canonicalLoanId, @RequestParam("ts") Instant ts) {
        return versionRepo.findAsOf(canonicalLoanId, ts)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("No version as of ts"));
    }
}
```

# F) AI Usage (Full-fledged, but **deterministic + controlled**)

LoanData Canon is **not** a “black box AI truth generator.” It is a **governed kernel**. AI is used only where it is defensible:

1. **Document parsing assistance (human-in-the-loop)**
   - AI extracts candidate fields from PDFs (facility agreements, amendments).
   - Output is **proposal only** → requires approval and creates a version with `reason_code=AI_PROPOSAL_APPROVED`.
   - Lineage stores model id + prompt version as `transform_rule_id`.

2. **Anomaly detection (control signal)**
   - AI flags outliers: principal jumps, maturity regressions, sudden currency changes.
   - It does **not** modify values—only creates `validation_event`/alerts.

3. **Mapping suggestion**
   - When new source tape columns arrive, AI suggests mapping to canonical fields.
   - Final mapping is stored as deterministic config and version-controlled.

**Regulator-safe rule:** canonical values are produced by **rules + approvals**, not model guesses. AI produces *candidates and alerts*, never silent writes.

# G) Manual Testing (100% step-by-step)

## G0. Preconditions
- PostgreSQL running with schema applied.
- Spring Boot app running on `localhost:8080`.
- A test JWT or basic auth configured (if security enabled). For now assume internal network or dev auth.

## G1. Positive case: ingest new loan
**Action (curl):**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/api/v1/ingest/loan \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "sourceSystem":"SERVICER_A",
    "sourceMessageId":"msg-0001",
    "sourceRef":"servicer_export_2026-01-12.csv#row=10",
    "createdBy":"svc-ingestor",
    "sourceLoanIdentifier":"SVCA-LOAN-8891",
    "effectiveTs":"2026-01-12T01:00:00Z",
    "originalPrincipal":1000000.00,
    "principalOutstanding":950000.00,
    "currency":"GBP",
    "interestRateType":"FLOATING",
    "baseRateIndex":"SONIA",
    "spreadBps":275,
    "maturityDate":"2030-12-31",
    "paymentFrequency":"QUARTERLY",
    "dayCountConvention":"ACT/365",
    "borrowerLei":"5493001KJTIIGC8Y1R12",
    "facilityType":"TERM_LOAN",
    "governingLaw":"ENGLAND_WALES"
  }'
```

**Expected result:**
- HTTP 200 JSON: `status=PROCESSED`, returns `canonicalLoanId`, `versionNo=1`.
- DB:
  - `canonical_loan` has 1 row
  - `loan_identifier` has 1 row
  - `loan_version` has 1 row
  - `loan_field_lineage` has rows for fields written
  - `ingestion_receipt` status PROCESSED
  - `outbox_event` contains LoanVersionPublished event

## G2. Query current snapshot
```bash
curl http://localhost:8080/api/v1/loans/<canonicalLoanId>/current
```
**Expected:**
- Returns latest version JSON with principalOutstanding=950000.

## G3. Idempotency: replay same message
Repeat G1 same payload.
**Expected:**
- HTTP 200 JSON `status=DUPLICATE_IGNORED`
- No new `loan_version` created.

## G4. Negative case: invalid currency
Send currency `"GBPX"`.
**Expected:**
- HTTP 400 with error code `VALIDATION_FAILED`
- `ingestion_receipt` status REJECTED
- `validation_event` row inserted

## G5. Negative case: outstanding > original
Set `principalOutstanding=1200000`, `originalPrincipal=1000000`.
**Expected:** rejected with `VALIDATION_OUTSTANDING_GT_ORIGINAL`.

## G6. Edge case: change originalPrincipal without override
1) Ingest v1 as above.  
2) Ingest v2 with same identifier but `originalPrincipal=1100000` and no `overrideReason`.
**Expected:** HTTP 400 with `IMMUTABLE_FIELD_CHANGE_REQUIRES_OVERRIDE`, no new version.

## G7. Edge case: change originalPrincipal with override
Repeat v2 but include `"overrideReason":"LEGAL_AMENDMENT_2026_01_10_APPROVED"`.
**Expected:** versionNo=2 created, lineage recorded, outbox event created.

```java
package com.bank.ldc.unit;

import com.bank.ldc.dto.IngestLoanRequest;
import com.bank.ldc.service.ValidationService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class ValidationServiceTest {

    @Test
    void floatingRequiresBaseRateAndSpread() {
        ValidationService svc = new ValidationService();
        IngestLoanRequest r = baseReq();
        r.interestRateType = "FLOATING";
        r.baseRateIndex = null;
        r.spreadBps = null;

        var errors = svc.validate(r);
        assertTrue(errors.contains("VALIDATION_BASE_RATE_REQUIRED"));
        assertTrue(errors.contains("VALIDATION_SPREAD_REQUIRED"));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsOutstandingGreaterThanOriginal() {
        ValidationService svc = new ValidationService();
        IngestLoanRequest r = baseReq();
        r.originalPrincipal = new BigDecimal("100.00");
        r.principalOutstanding = new BigDecimal("200.00");

        var errors = svc.validate(r);
        assertTrue(errors.contains("VALIDATION_OUTSTANDING_GT_ORIGINAL"));
    }

    private IngestLoanRequest baseReq() {
        IngestLoanRequest r = new IngestLoanRequest();
        r.sourceSystem="S";
        r.sourceMessageId="M";
        r.sourceRef="R";
        r.createdBy="U";
        r.sourceLoanIdentifier="ID";
        r.effectiveTs= Instant.parse("2026-01-12T01:00:00Z");
        r.originalPrincipal=new BigDecimal("1000.00");
        r.principalOutstanding=new BigDecimal("900.00");
        r.currency="GBP";
        r.interestRateType="FIXED";
        r.maturityDate= LocalDate.parse("2030-12-31");
        r.paymentFrequency="QUARTERLY";
        r.dayCountConvention="ACT/365";
        r.facilityType="TERM_LOAN";
        return r;
    }
}
```

# J) Cross-Product Contracts (What LoanData Canon emits/consumed)

## J1. Emits (Downstream consumption)
**Event type:** `LoanVersionPublished`  
**Aggregate:** `CanonicalLoan`  
**Payload (JSON schema):**
```json
{
  "eventType": "LoanVersionPublished",
  "canonicalLoanId": "uuid",
  "loanVersionId": "uuid",
  "versionNo": 2,
  "publishedAt": "2026-01-12T01:10:00Z"
}
```

**Downstream usage**
- **CovenantPulse** subscribes to recompute covenant tests on new versions.
- **GreenLedger** subscribes to compute ESG classification and green tagging.
- **TradeReady** subscribes to build accurate trade tapes (as-of versions).
- **PortView** subscribes to portfolio aggregation from consistent snapshots.

## J2. Exposes read contracts (APIs)
- `GET /api/v1/loans/{canonicalLoanId}/current`
- `GET /api/v1/loans/{canonicalLoanId}/asof?ts=...`

These are the **only sanctioned ways** downstream reads the canonical loan snapshot (no direct DB reads).

## E5) Security Layer (Regulator-safe)

### Security requirements satisfied
- R-LDC-08 (Security + PII)
- SOX / PRA / FCA audit expectation
- Clear separation: INGEST vs READ vs AUDIT

### Roles
| Role | Permissions |
|---|---|
| `ROLE_LDC_INGEST` | POST ingest |
| `ROLE_LDC_READ` | Read current/as-of |
| `ROLE_LDC_AUDIT` | Lineage, diff, evidence |
| `ROLE_LDC_ADMIN` | All |

### SecurityConfig.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.config;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;

@Configuration
public class SecurityConfig {

    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain filterChain(HttpSecurity http) throws Exception {
        http
          .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())
          .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth
              .requestMatchers("/api/v1/ingest/**").hasRole("LDC_INGEST")
              .requestMatchers("/api/v1/loans/**").hasAnyRole("LDC_READ","LDC_AUDIT","LDC_ADMIN")
              .requestMatchers("/api/v1/audit/**").hasAnyRole("LDC_AUDIT","LDC_ADMIN")
              .anyRequest().denyAll()
          )
          .httpBasic(); // internal trusted network / replace with OAuth2 in prod

        return http.build();
    }
}
```

**Why this matters**
- Auditors expect **hard permission boundaries**
- Prevents “ops accidentally mutating history”
- Prevents developers querying audit data without justification

## E6) Repository Layer (Complete, No Assumptions)

### LoanVersionRepository.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.repo;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.LoanVersion;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.*;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

public interface LoanVersionRepository extends JpaRepository<LoanVersion, UUID> {

    @Query("""
        select max(l.versionNo)
        from LoanVersion l
        where l.canonicalLoanId = :loanId
    """)
    Optional<Integer> findMaxVersionNo(@Param("loanId") UUID loanId);

    @Query("""
        select l
        from LoanVersion l
        where l.canonicalLoanId = :loanId
        order by l.versionNo desc
        limit 1
    """)
    Optional<LoanVersion> findLatest(@Param("loanId") UUID loanId);

    @Query("""
        select l
        from LoanVersion l
        where l.canonicalLoanId = :loanId
          and l.effectiveTs <= :ts
        order by l.effectiveTs desc
        limit 1
    """)
    Optional<LoanVersion> findAsOf(@Param("loanId") UUID loanId, @Param("ts") Instant ts);
}
```

## E7) Audit & Evidence APIs (Regulator Core)

### What auditors actually ask
- “Why is this value X?”
- “What changed since last month?”
- “Who approved the change?”
- “What source document supports this?”

We answer **deterministically**.

---

### AuditController.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.api;

import com.bank.ldc.repo.*;
import com.bank.ldc.service.DiffService;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.UUID;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/audit")
public class AuditController {

    private final LoanVersionRepository versionRepo;
    private final LoanFieldLineageRepository lineageRepo;
    private final DiffService diffService;

    public AuditController(
        LoanVersionRepository versionRepo,
        LoanFieldLineageRepository lineageRepo,
        DiffService diffService
    ) {
        this.versionRepo = versionRepo;
        this.lineageRepo = lineageRepo;
        this.diffService = diffService;
    }

    /** Why is this value what it is? */
    @GetMapping("/loan-version/{loanVersionId}/lineage")
    public Object lineage(@PathVariable UUID loanVersionId) {
        return lineageRepo.findByLoanVersionId(loanVersionId);
    }

    /** What changed between two versions? */
    @GetMapping("/loan/{canonicalLoanId}/diff")
    public Object diff(
        @PathVariable UUID canonicalLoanId,
        @RequestParam int fromVersion,
        @RequestParam int toVersion
    ) {
        return diffService.diff(canonicalLoanId, fromVersion, toVersion);
    }
}
```

## E8) Diff Engine (Field-level, explainable)

### DiffService.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.LoanVersion;
import com.bank.ldc.repo.LoanVersionRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.*;

@Service
public class DiffService {

    private final LoanVersionRepository repo;

    public DiffService(LoanVersionRepository repo) {
        this.repo = repo;
    }

    public Map<String, Object> diff(UUID loanId, int fromV, int toV) {
        LoanVersion from = repo.findAll().stream()
            .filter(v -> v.getCanonicalLoanId().equals(loanId) && v.getVersionNo() == fromV)
            .findFirst().orElseThrow();

        LoanVersion to = repo.findAll().stream()
            .filter(v -> v.getCanonicalLoanId().equals(loanId) && v.getVersionNo() == toV)
            .findFirst().orElseThrow();

        Map<String,Object> changes = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        for (Field f : LoanVersion.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
            f.setAccessible(true);
            try {
                Object a = f.get(from);
                Object b = f.get(to);
                if (!Objects.equals(a, b)) {
                    changes.put(f.getName(), Map.of("from", a, "to", b));
                }
            } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
                throw new RuntimeException(e);
            }
        }
        return changes;
    }
}
```

**Why this passes audit**
- No AI
- No heuristics
- Pure reflection diff
- Reproducible anytime

---

## E9) Outbox Publisher (Exactly-once)

### Why required
- Regulators hate “event lost / duplicated”
- Secondary trading **cannot tolerate drift**

---

### OutboxPublisher.java
```java
package com.bank.ldc.service;

import com.bank.ldc.domain.OutboxEvent;
import com.bank.ldc.repo.OutboxEventRepository;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

@Component
public class OutboxPublisher {

    private final OutboxEventRepository repo;

    public OutboxPublisher(OutboxEventRepository repo) {
        this.repo = repo;
    }

    @Scheduled(fixedDelay = 5000)
    public void publish() {
        List<OutboxEvent> events = repo.findUnpublished(50);

        for (OutboxEvent e : events) {
            try {
                // send to Kafka / EventBridge / MQ (mocked here)
                // kafka.send(e.getPayloadJson())

                e.markPublished(Instant.now());
                repo.save(e);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                e.incrementAttempts();
                repo.save(e);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

**Guarantees**
- Write canonical version + event in **same transaction**
- Publish retries are safe
- Consumers can dedupe on `event_id`

# A) Business Reality — Covenant Monitoring (Real Market Pain)

## A1. Exact problem statement
Loan covenants (financial & non-financial) are **legally binding control mechanisms**, yet in most institutions they are:

- Parsed from **PDFs / Word docs / emails**
- Re-keyed into spreadsheets
- Calculated monthly or quarterly **after the fact**
- Reviewed manually by credit officers

This causes **late breach detection**, disputes with borrowers, and regulatory exposure.

**CovenantPulse** is a **deterministic covenant evaluation engine** that:
- Converts legal covenant language into **machine-executable rules**
- Evaluates covenants **as-of any LoanData Canon version**
- Produces **explainable breach evidence**, not just flags

---

## A2. Why this pain exists today
1. **Covenants live in documents**, not systems  
   → PDFs have narrative clauses, thresholds, carve-outs.

2. **Ambiguous calculation logic**  
   → EBITDA definition differs per deal; add-backs vary.

3. **Timing mismatch**  
   → Financials arrive quarterly; loan balances change daily.

4. **Manual overrides without lineage**  
   → Waivers and amendments are not versioned properly.

5. **No canonical “as-of” anchor**  
   → Covenant teams argue with finance about which numbers were used.

---

## A3. Who suffers
- **Credit Risk / Portfolio Managers** – late breach discovery
- **Relationship Managers** – borrower disputes
- **Legal** – covenant interpretation conflicts
- **Regulators / Internal Audit** – weak controls, no evidence trail
- **Secondary Market Buyers** – undisclosed covenant stress

---

## A4. Quantified impact
Typical mid-large lender:
- **5–15% of breaches detected late**
- **Weeks of manual work** per amendment / waiver
- **Material mis-pricing risk** in secondary trades
- **Audit findings** around covenant governance

---

# B) Enterprise Requirements (Numbered, Non-Negotiable)

> Prefix: **R-CP-XX**

---

### R-CP-01 Canonical Covenant Definition
**Requirement**  
System shall store covenants as **structured, executable definitions**.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Covenant definition stored as versioned JSON / DSL
- Each covenant explicitly references:
  - metric definition
  - comparison operator
  - threshold(s)
  - frequency
- Legal text preserved alongside executable form

---

### R-CP-02 Covenant Versioning
**Requirement**  
Covenants shall be immutable and versioned.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Amendments create new covenant version
- Prior versions remain queryable
- Each version has effective date and approval metadata

---

### R-CP-03 Deterministic Metric Resolution
**Requirement**  
Metrics used in covenants must resolve deterministically.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Metrics resolved from:
  - LoanData Canon versions
  - Financial statement inputs
- Metric formula is explicit and stored
- Same inputs always produce same value

---

### R-CP-04 As-Of Evaluation
**Requirement**  
Covenants shall be evaluable **as-of any timestamp**.

**Acceptance criteria**
- System selects correct:
  - Loan version
  - Covenant version
  - Financial statement period
- Output reproducible for audit

---

### R-CP-05 Breach Detection
**Requirement**  
System shall classify covenant state.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Status ∈ {COMPLIANT, BREACH, CURED, WAIVED}
- Breach date calculated deterministically
- Severity calculated if defined

---

### R-CP-06 Waivers & Cures
**Requirement**  
System shall support waivers and cures.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Waiver references covenant version
- Waiver has validity window
- Waiver does NOT delete breach history

---

### R-CP-07 Explainability
**Requirement**  
System shall explain covenant outcomes.

**Acceptance criteria**
- For any evaluation:
  - input values
  - formula used
  - threshold
  - comparison result
- Human-readable + machine output

---

### R-CP-08 Event Emission
**Requirement**  
Breach and state changes shall emit events.

**Acceptance criteria**
- Events emitted transactionally
- Downstream products can subscribe
- Exactly-once semantics supported

---

### R-CP-09 Audit Evidence
**Requirement**  
System shall provide regulator-ready evidence.

**Acceptance criteria**
- “Why breach?” endpoint
- “What changed?” between periods
- Immutable history preserved

---

### R-CP-10 Performance
**Requirement**  
Portfolio-level evaluation must scale.

**Acceptance criteria**
- 10k loans × covenants evaluated < SLA batch window
- Deterministic batch execution

# C) Domain Model — CovenantPulse (Full entity list + fields + why + what breaks)

## C1. Entity list
1. **CovenantPackage** (per facility/loan scope)
2. **CovenantDefinition** (the covenant “type” / clause family)
3. **CovenantVersion** (immutable executable rule + legal text)
4. **MetricDefinition** (deterministic metric formula)
5. **MetricVersion** (immutable formula versioning)
6. **FinancialStatement** (borrower financial inputs per period)
7. **FinancialStatementLine** (line items: Revenue, EBITDA, etc.)
8. **CovenantEvaluationRun** (a run/job instance)
9. **CovenantEvaluationResult** (result per covenant per period/as-of)
10. **CovenantResultEvidence** (inputs, formula, threshold, comparison trace)
11. **CovenantWaiver** (waiver window + authority)
12. **CovenantCure** (cure action + date)
13. **OutboxEvent** (transactional event emission)
14. **IngestionReceipt** (idempotent ingestion of financials/definitions)

> **Design principle:** Everything legally sensitive is **versioned + immutable**. Operational statuses evolve via new records, not overwrites.

---

## C2. Field-level explanation (core entities)

### 1) CovenantPackage
Represents a set of covenants attached to a **canonical loan / facility**.
- `package_id (UUID)` — primary key  
- `canonical_loan_id (UUID)` — join to Product-1 identity  
- `scope_type` — LOAN / FACILITY / BORROWER / GROUP  
- `status` — ACTIVE / INACTIVE  
- `created_at/by`  
**Why exists:** covenants are applied as a bundle per deal.  
**If missing:** you cannot manage amendments cleanly per deal.

### 2) CovenantDefinition
Non-versioned identity of a covenant concept.
- `definition_id`
- `code` (e.g., `LEV_RATIO_MAX`, `ICR_MIN`)
- `name`
- `category` (FINANCIAL/NON_FINANCIAL)
**Why:** stable identity for versioning.  
**If missing:** amendments break references.

### 3) CovenantVersion (immutable rule)
- `covenant_version_id`
- `definition_id`, `package_id`
- `version_no`
- `effective_from`, `effective_to`
- `legal_text` (original clause snippet)
- `dsl_json` (executable rule)
- `approval_status` (DRAFT/APPROVED)
- `approved_by/at`
**Why:** legal changes create new versions.  
**If missing:** cannot reproduce “as-of” covenant logic.

### 4) MetricDefinition + MetricVersion
Deterministic computation components.
- `metric_code` (e.g., `EBITDA`, `NET_DEBT`, `LEV_RATIO`)
- `metric_version_no`
- `formula_json` (explicit operator tree)
- `dependencies` (other metric codes)
- `effective_from/to`
**Why:** EBITDA definitions vary per deal; must be explicit.  
**If missing:** covenant calculation becomes ambiguous → disputes.

### 5) FinancialStatement (+ lines)
- `statement_id`, `borrower_id/lei`, `period_start/end`, `currency`
- `source_ref`, `received_at/by`
- Lines: `line_code`, `amount`, `unit`, `notes`
**Why:** covenants often depend on quarterly/annual financials.  
**If missing:** covenant evaluation becomes impossible or manual.

### 6) CovenantEvaluationResult + Evidence
- `result_id`, `run_id`, `package_id`, `covenant_version_id`
- `as_of_ts` (ties to LoanData Canon version selection)
- `period_end` (financial period)
- `status` {COMPLIANT, BREACH, CURED, WAIVED}
- `measured_value`, `threshold_value`, `operator`
- Evidence: `inputs_json`, `formula_trace_json`, `threshold_trace_json`, `loan_version_id`, `metric_versions_used`
**Why:** regulators demand explainability.  
**If missing:** cannot defend breach calls.

### 7) Waiver/Cure
- `waiver_id`, `covenant_version_id`, `valid_from/to`, `authority`, `doc_ref`
- Cure: `cure_id`, `breach_result_id`, `cure_date`, `details`
**Why:** legal reality requires waivers/cures while preserving breach history.  
**If missing:** inaccurate status & audit failures.

---

# D) Database Layer — Full DDL (PostgreSQL)

```sql
CREATE TABLE cp_covenant_package (
  package_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  canonical_loan_id UUID NOT NULL,
  scope_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE cp_covenant_definition (
  definition_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  category VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE cp_metric_definition (
  metric_definition_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE cp_metric_version (
  metric_version_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  metric_definition_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_metric_definition(metric_definition_id),
  version_no INT NOT NULL,
  effective_from DATE NOT NULL,
  effective_to DATE NULL,
  formula_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  approval_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  approved_by VARCHAR(100) NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_metric_version UNIQUE(metric_definition_id, version_no)
);

CREATE TABLE cp_covenant_version (
  covenant_version_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  package_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_covenant_package(package_id),
  definition_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_covenant_definition(definition_id),
  version_no INT NOT NULL,
  effective_from DATE NOT NULL,
  effective_to DATE NULL,
  legal_text TEXT NOT NULL,
  dsl_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  approval_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
  approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  approved_by VARCHAR(100) NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_cov_version UNIQUE(package_id, definition_id, version_no)
);

CREATE TABLE cp_financial_statement (
  statement_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  borrower_lei VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  period_start DATE NOT NULL,
  period_end DATE NOT NULL,
  currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,
  source_ref VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  received_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_stmt UNIQUE(borrower_lei, period_end, source_ref)
);

CREATE TABLE cp_financial_statement_line (
  line_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  statement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_financial_statement(statement_id),
  line_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  amount NUMERIC(20,2) NOT NULL,
  unit VARCHAR(20) NULL,
  notes TEXT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT uq_stmt_line UNIQUE(statement_id, line_code)
);

CREATE TABLE cp_eval_run (
  run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  package_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_covenant_package(package_id),
  as_of_ts TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  requested_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- STARTED/COMPLETED/FAILED
  error_code VARCHAR(50) NULL
);

CREATE TABLE cp_eval_result (
  result_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_eval_run(run_id),
  package_id UUID NOT NULL,
  covenant_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_covenant_version(covenant_version_id),
  as_of_ts TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  period_end DATE NOT NULL,
  status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- COMPLIANT/BREACH/CURED/WAIVED
  measured_value NUMERIC(20,6) NULL,
  threshold_value NUMERIC(20,6) NULL,
  operator VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
  breach_detected_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT uq_result UNIQUE(covenant_version_id, as_of_ts, period_end)
);

CREATE TABLE cp_result_evidence (
  evidence_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  result_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_eval_result(result_id),
  loan_version_id UUID NOT NULL, -- from LoanData Canon
  metric_versions_used JSONB NOT NULL,
  inputs_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  formula_trace_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  threshold_trace_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE cp_waiver (
  waiver_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  covenant_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_covenant_version(covenant_version_id),
  valid_from DATE NOT NULL,
  valid_to DATE NOT NULL,
  authority VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  doc_ref VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE cp_cure (
  cure_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  result_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cp_eval_result(result_id),
  cure_date DATE NOT NULL,
  details TEXT NOT NULL,
  doc_ref VARCHAR(200) NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

-- outbox copied pattern from Product 1 (separate table is ok)
CREATE TABLE cp_outbox_event (
  event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
  event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
  aggregate_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
  aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,
  payload_json JSONB NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL,
  publish_attempts INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE INDEX idx_cov_effective ON cp_covenant_version(package_id, effective_from DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_metric_effective ON cp_metric_version(metric_definition_id, effective_from DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_eval_lookup ON cp_eval_result(package_id, as_of_ts DESC, period_end DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_unpublished_cp ON cp_outbox_event(published_at) WHERE published_at IS NULL;
```

### Immutable vs mutable
- **Immutable append-only:** `cp_covenant_version`, `cp_metric_version`, `cp_eval_result`, `cp_result_evidence`, `cp_outbox_event`
- **Mutable:** `cp_covenant_package.status`, `cp_eval_run.status` (operational)
- **Overrides/waivers/cures:** appended records (`cp_waiver`, `cp_cure`), never overwriting evaluation history

---

# E) Service Layer (CODE) — Spring Boot Enterprise Kernel

## E1. DSL Contract (Executable Covenant Rule)
We use JSON DSL to avoid “free text logic”.

**Example covenant DSL (Leverage Ratio max 4.0x):**
```json
{
  "metric": "LEV_RATIO",
  "operator": "<=",
  "threshold": { "type": "constant", "value": 4.0 },
  "frequency": "QUARTERLY",
  "grace_days": 0
}
```

**Metric formula JSON (Net Debt / EBITDA):**
```json
{
  "op": "divide",
  "left": { "metric": "NET_DEBT" },
  "right": { "metric": "EBITDA" }
}
```

This ensures determinism + auditability.

---

## E2. Core responsibilities (classes)
- `CovenantEvaluationService` — orchestrates evaluation as-of
- `MetricEngine` — computes metrics deterministically with trace
- `CovenantRuleEngine` — applies operator/threshold with evidence
- `WaiverCureService` — transforms BREACH → WAIVED/CURED while preserving history
- `OutboxService` — emits breach/state events
- `AsOfResolver` — selects correct covenant/metric versions for timestamp/period

---

## E3. MetricEngine (deterministic + traceable)
```java
package com.bank.cp.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.MathContext;
import java.util.*;

public class MetricEngine {

    public record MetricResult(BigDecimal value, Map<String,Object> trace) {}

    private final MetricRepository metricRepository; // loads approved metric versions + formulas
    private final FinancialsRepository financialsRepository; // loads statement lines

    public MetricEngine(MetricRepository metricRepository, FinancialsRepository financialsRepository) {
        this.metricRepository = metricRepository;
        this.financialsRepository = financialsRepository;
    }

    public MetricResult compute(String borrowerLei, String metricCode, DateRange period, Set<String> stack) {
        if (stack.contains(metricCode)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cyclic metric dependency: " + metricCode);
        }
        stack.add(metricCode);

        var metricVersion = metricRepository.loadApprovedMetricVersion(metricCode, period.end());
        var formula = metricVersion.formulaJson();

        // Evaluate formula tree with trace
        MetricResult r = evalNode(borrowerLei, formula, period, stack);

        Map<String,Object> trace = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        trace.put("metricCode", metricCode);
        trace.put("metricVersionNo", metricVersion.versionNo());
        trace.put("effectiveFrom", metricVersion.effectiveFrom());
        trace.put("formula", formula);
        trace.put("result", r.value());
        trace.put("subtrace", r.trace());

        return new MetricResult(r.value(), trace);
    }

    private MetricResult evalNode(String borrowerLei, Map<String,Object> node, DateRange period, Set<String> stack) {
        if (node.containsKey("metric")) {
            String code = (String) node.get("metric");

            // If this metric is a base line item, pull from financial statement lines
            if (financialsRepository.isBaseLine(code)) {
                BigDecimal v = financialsRepository.getLineAmount(borrowerLei, period.end(), code);
                return new MetricResult(v, Map.of("baseLine", code, "value", v));
            }

            MetricResult nested = compute(borrowerLei, code, period, stack);
            return new MetricResult(nested.value(), Map.of("metric", code, "trace", nested.trace()));
        }

        String op = (String) node.get("op");
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String,Object> left = (Map<String,Object>) node.get("left");
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String,Object> right = (Map<String,Object>) node.get("right");

        MetricResult l = evalNode(borrowerLei, left, period, stack);
        MetricResult r = evalNode(borrowerLei, right, period, stack);

        BigDecimal out;
        switch (op) {
            case "add" -> out = l.value().add(r.value());
            case "subtract" -> out = l.value().subtract(r.value());
            case "multiply" -> out = l.value().multiply(r.value());
            case "divide" -> out = l.value().divide(r.value(), new MathContext(16));
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown op: " + op);
        }

        Map<String,Object> trace = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        trace.put("op", op);
        trace.put("left", l.trace());
        trace.put("right", r.trace());
        trace.put("value", out);

        return new MetricResult(out, trace);
    }

    public record DateRange(java.time.LocalDate start, java.time.LocalDate end) {}
}
```

**Failure modes**
- Cyclic dependency → hard fail (control breach)
- Missing base line item → fail with explicit error code (not “0” silently)
- Divide by zero → fail (or explicit policy)

---

## E4. CovenantRuleEngine (operator + threshold + evidence)
```java
package com.bank.cp.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.*;

public class CovenantRuleEngine {

    public record CovenantOutcome(String status, Map<String,Object> evidence) {}

    public CovenantOutcome evaluate(BigDecimal measured, String operator, BigDecimal threshold) {
        boolean ok;
        switch (operator) {
            case "<=" -> ok = measured.compareTo(threshold) <= 0;
            case "<"  -> ok = measured.compareTo(threshold) < 0;
            case ">=" -> ok = measured.compareTo(threshold) >= 0;
            case ">"  -> ok = measured.compareTo(threshold) > 0;
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid operator: " + operator);
        }

        Map<String,Object> ev = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        ev.put("measured", measured);
        ev.put("operator", operator);
        ev.put("threshold", threshold);
        ev.put("comparisonResult", ok);

        return new CovenantOutcome(ok ? "COMPLIANT" : "BREACH", ev);
    }
}
```

---

## E5. CovenantEvaluationService (as-of resolver + version selection + result write + outbox)
```java
package com.bank.cp.service;

import com.bank.cp.repo.*;
import com.bank.cp.domain.*;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.*;
import java.util.*;

@Service
public class CovenantEvaluationService {

    private final CovenantRepo covenantRepo;
    private final PackageRepo packageRepo;
    private final FinancialsRepo financialsRepo;
    private final EvidenceRepo evidenceRepo;
    private final ResultRepo resultRepo;
    private final RunRepo runRepo;
    private final OutboxService outboxService;

    private final LoanDataCanonClient ldcClient; // Product-1 contract client
    private final MetricEngine metricEngine;
    private final CovenantRuleEngine ruleEngine;

    public CovenantEvaluationService(
        CovenantRepo covenantRepo,
        PackageRepo packageRepo,
        FinancialsRepo financialsRepo,
        EvidenceRepo evidenceRepo,
        ResultRepo resultRepo,
        RunRepo runRepo,
        OutboxService outboxService,
        LoanDataCanonClient ldcClient,
        MetricEngine metricEngine,
        CovenantRuleEngine ruleEngine
    ) {
        this.covenantRepo = covenantRepo;
        this.packageRepo = packageRepo;
        this.financialsRepo = financialsRepo;
        this.evidenceRepo = evidenceRepo;
        this.resultRepo = resultRepo;
        this.runRepo = runRepo;
        this.outboxService = outboxService;
        this.ldcClient = ldcClient;
        this.metricEngine = metricEngine;
        this.ruleEngine = ruleEngine;
    }

    @Transactional
    public UUID runPackageEvaluation(UUID packageId, Instant asOfTs, LocalDate periodEnd, String requestedBy) {
        CovenantPackage pkg = packageRepo.getActive(packageId);

        UUID runId = UUID.randomUUID();
        runRepo.insertRun(runId, packageId, asOfTs, requestedBy);

        // Resolve loan snapshot as-of (Product 1)
        var loanSnapshot = ldcClient.getLoanAsOf(pkg.getCanonicalLoanId(), asOfTs);
        UUID loanVersionId = loanSnapshot.loanVersionId();

        // Get applicable covenant versions (approved, effective for periodEnd)
        List<CovenantVersion> covs = covenantRepo.findApprovedEffective(pkg.getPackageId(), periodEnd);

        for (CovenantVersion cv : covs) {
            // Parse DSL
            var dsl = cv.getDslJson();
            String metricCode = dsl.metric();
            String operator = dsl.operator();
            java.math.BigDecimal threshold = dsl.thresholdConstant();

            // Resolve borrower & period financials
            String borrowerLei = loanSnapshot.borrowerLei();
            if (!financialsRepo.existsStatement(borrowerLei, periodEnd)) {
                // control: write FAILED result or explicit BREACH? Here: FAIL with evidence event
                runRepo.fail(runId, "MISSING_FINANCIALS");
                throw new IllegalStateException("Missing financial statement for borrower " + borrowerLei + " periodEnd " + periodEnd);
            }

            var metric = metricEngine.compute(borrowerLei, metricCode, new MetricEngine.DateRange(periodEnd.minusMonths(3), periodEnd), new HashSet<>());
            var outcome = ruleEngine.evaluate(metric.value(), operator, threshold);

            UUID resultId = UUID.randomUUID();
            resultRepo.insertResult(resultId, runId, pkg.getPackageId(), cv.getCovenantVersionId(),
                    asOfTs, periodEnd, outcome.status(), metric.value(), threshold, operator,
                    outcome.status().equals("BREACH") ? Instant.now() : null);

            UUID evidenceId = UUID.randomUUID();
            evidenceRepo.insertEvidence(evidenceId, resultId, loanVersionId,
                    Map.of("metricVersions", metric.trace().get("metricVersionNo")),
                    Map.of("borrowerLei", borrowerLei, "periodEnd", periodEnd.toString()),
                    metric.trace(),
                    Map.of("threshold", threshold, "operator", operator));

            // Apply waiver/cure transformations AFTER storing baseline result (immutable)
            String finalStatus = resultRepo.applyWaiverAndCure(resultId, cv.getCovenantVersionId(), periodEnd);

            // Emit events (transactional)
            outboxService.emitCovenantEvaluated(pkg.getCanonicalLoanId(), cv.getCovenantVersionId(), resultId, finalStatus, asOfTs, periodEnd);
            if (finalStatus.equals("BREACH")) {
                outboxService.emitCovenantBreached(pkg.getCanonicalLoanId(), cv.getCovenantVersionId(), resultId, asOfTs, periodEnd);
            }
        }

        runRepo.complete(runId);
        return runId;
    }
}
```

**Failure modes**
- Missing financials → run fails with explicit code
- Missing loan snapshot → run fails
- Invalid DSL → run fails & error event
- DB constraints prevent duplicates (idempotent unique keys on result)

# F) AI Usage (Full-fledged but controlled)

CovenantPulse can use AI in **three governed** areas:

1. **Clause-to-DSL extraction (proposal only)**
   - Input: legal text clause
   - Output: candidate DSL + metric mapping suggestions
   - Must go through **approval workflow** → only approved version enters `cp_covenant_version` with `approval_status=APPROVED`.

2. **Metric mapping assistant**
   - Suggest mapping from borrower statement line items to base metrics.
   - Final mapping stored deterministically as config.

3. **Anomaly alerts**
   - Detect suspicious swings in EBITDA/Net Debt ratios.
   - Creates alert events, does not alter results.

**Hard rule:** AI never silently changes covenant outcomes. It can only propose and alert.

GreenLedger is the **regulator/auditor-safe ESG & Green Lending runtime** that sits on top of **LoanData Canon (Product 1)** and (optionally) **CovenantPulse (Product 2)**. It produces **immutable ESG facts, classifications, evidence packs, and disclosure-grade outputs**.
